ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u250	Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! I would like to present the position of the Ministry of Environment vis-à-vis the draft law amending the Law on Hunting, print No 219. The Parliamentary bill amending the Law on Hunting Law undoubtedly meets the demands of the agricultural environment related to the growing problem of hunting damages in recent years. Therefore, the solutions proposed to involve more of the State Treasury's contribution to the payment of compensation and the proposals for changes to the entities assessing the damage deserve directional support. However, the proposal requires not only far-reaching legislative and editorial amendments but, above all, rethinking the validity of the concrete solutions proposed. In particular, it should be noted that the proposed amendment does not provide the necessary procedural guarantees. As the first President of the Supreme Court rightly pointed out in his opinion, the draft does not specify whether proceedings to determine the amount of compensation would be carried out under the provisions of the Code of Administrative Procedure. or under some separate procedure. It seems that this was a specific procedure for the Code of Administrative Procedure, and given that the proposed regulations concern the spending of public and private funds, the rules should clearly and transparently define this procedure. Moreover, the project raises doubts as to the extent to which it provides for a single instance of proceedings to determine the amount of compensation, while there are no precise arrangements for the review by the general courts. Following the General Data Protection Officer, it should be pointed out that the proposed regulations need to be clarified in order to ensure compliance with the statutory requirements for the protection of personal data, in particular as regards the protocol on the inspection and assessment of damage and the list of experts. It is necessary to supplement the regulations on the proposed lists of experts, including the tightening of requirements for candidates, which have been raised in their opinions, among others. National Council of Agricultural Chambers and the Union of Rural Communities. The project does not specify the criteria according to which the amount of compensation is to be determined. The project also raises serious concerns as to the rules governing the functioning of a possible compensation fund, including the clarification of the form of operation of the fund - it is not clear whether it is a State special-purpose fund, within the meaning of the Public Finance Act or any other form - to ensure the financial liquidity of the fund and to clearly distinguish the costs financed by that fund and the costs covered by the State budget. What is equally important, despite the projected far-reaching financial implications for the state budget - here they are estimated at about PLN 55 million per year - neither the basis for the estimates made nor the potential sources of funding them. Finally, the project does not contain the necessary transitional provisions. The above reservations, as well as a number of other doubts raised to this project, make the implementation of the proposed solutions in the form of this draft law in practice possible to block the procedures for estimating and paying compensation. As a result, it will be necessary for the legislator to intervene again as a matter of urgency. It is also impossible to predict what kind and to what extent the consequences of such a blockade will have to be borne by the State Treasury. For these reasons, I request that the draft be referred to the committee for consideration. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u268	Thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! Work on the 2016 budget is coming to an end. I have the honour to present, on behalf of the Parliamentary Club, the position of the club with regard to the Senate resolution on the budget law, Seym printing Nos 251 and 257. Law and Justice supports all amendments tabled by the Senate when taking into account the position of the Committee on Public Finance. Finally, we will vote on these amendments in the plenary room. Work on the bill went smoothly from the very beginning. Despite numerous comments, the divergences of both substantive committees and the Committee on Public Finances had sufficient time to follow closely the detailed provisions of the draft law. Despite the change of power, there is a full chance that the managers of individual budget parts will receive a ready document signed by President Andrzej Duda in a few days' time and proceed to the implementation of the expenditure by issuing invitations to tender, seizing public money to work as soon as possible, so that, for example, it is not necessary to invest on roads, bridge investments in late autumn under snow. They fought their unequal battles, faced with a hopeless situation, when from day to day, year to year, it became increasingly obvious that the only destiny was death, death experienced in battle, but also a methodical investigation every day, when incredibly brutality, cruelty and animalization were deprived of the cursed men of dignity, stripped of respect by fancy methods of rape, infliction of pain, liberation of fear. Today, when we take part in the running of the Wolf's trail, it is important for us to be able to see what a nightmare it was that had to go through in Rakowiecka, Montelupy, Lublin Castle or Bydgoszcz Fordon, young people at that time. Many of them 17-, 18-, 20-year-olds, at the beginning of their adult lives. They reached for weapons, entering the structures of the Freedom and Independence Association, the National Armed Forces, continued their fight in the AK ranks for free and independent Poland. Security was paned out - terror, fear, confidents were doing their jobs, arrests, denitions, spills. They went to prison as if they were inevitable. What happens in the soul of a 20-year-old young man who gets the death penalty, who sits on death row for a few months, when each key of the rattle is another goodbye to the world? What would be better? Then die in an investigation when they forced them to stand there, tearing apart the muscles of the anus, or crushing the genitals of the boots, boots, or ripping off the nails, or pushing splinters under them? Maybe if I had died then, I wouldn't have died every morning, so for months now... Some of them went out. Their abnormal world normalized, the scars on the body grew, but the soul constantly wept. You'd get up at night, scream, call your cellmates, and... you'd drink more and more. Sometimes a child, your child, from the door of his own room, listened to what you were saying, what you were talking about with those who found you - you mentioned it together. And then, despite what you've been through, what you've seen, you've taught them, you scumbag, the love of your country. You told me to believe in her, and when that moment came, to fight for her. Today, after many years, the Homeland opens its arms, takes you in zealously, re-finishes you, and even though its face brightens up the smile, tears are in its eyes, because many of you have lost forever, because too many have heard complaints of powerlessness expressed in lonely prison prayer. ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u385	Thank you. Mrs Bożena Kamińska, Civic Platform, has the floor.
ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u386	Thank you. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Once again, I would like to dedicate my statement to the lack of appropriate regulation which would determine the minimum distance of wind power plants from human habitats. In our country, renewable energy sources, including wind power plants, are increasingly promoted, leaving out the possibility of negative impacts. Local authorities are most in favour of building wind power plants on their land because of the financial benefits that enable them to make more of their own investments. The inevitability of the construction of wind power plants poses a challenge regarding their rational location. The Polish law to date does not specify what should be the right distance of the wind power plant from human habitats, and only the level of noise emitted is taken into account. This issue is one of the most disputed in the context of wind energy. In Western Europe, where wind energy has long been used, there are appropriate regulations that regulate the distance of wind power plants from human habitats. In France, for example, this distance from a single wind turbine is 1500 m, while in the Netherlands it is 2 000. m. There was also a strong resistance of the public to the construction of wind power plants. I come from Suwałszczyzna, where there are favorable areas and circumstances for these wind power plants to function, but society has shown great resistance to these power plants. People don't want to get near their homes of windmills generating noise, they don't want shadows thrown by windmill wings. The inevitability of the construction of wind power plants therefore poses a challenge regarding their rational location. Currently there are no definitions and terminology clarifications in the planning law, the Construction Law and the Real Estate Management Act. I believe that this issue needs to be addressed as soon as possible in order to introduce appropriate legislation that will bridge the gaps in the legislation. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u387	Thank you. Mr Paweł Szramka, Kukiz.15.

The winter season in Jakuszycka Poles starts from November and usually lasts until late spring. The snow cover is covered with a thick layer throughout the season. The highest point on the routes is 1000 m above sea level, and the lowest point is 752 m. The Piast Pole Association Centre is the only cross-country skiing centre in Poland, which has the worldwide approval of FIS competitive routes. Thanks to such wonderful conditions, the cross-country skiing centre in Yakuszyce has been organizing a cross-country skiing festival for amateurs for 40 years. The administrative authorities reported to their superiors on the disastrous situation of Communists in Podbeskidzie. This was confirmed by the events of May 3, 1946, when more than 100 armed and uniformed guerrillas under the command of the Flame captured the Vistula and organized the parade. As a result of the treason 150 soldiers were murdered, the remainder of the group appeared during the amnesty action in 1947. Henryk Flame was also among those exposed. Soon he divided the fate of his subordinates and was shot in Zabrzeg on 1 December 1947. The amnesty wasn't about heroes. Their sacrifice of life, effort and heroism did not go to waste. We live in a free country today. It's a great credit to the solid soldiers. ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u402	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! In 2015, the Council The RZGW provided some municipalities with flood and flood risk maps drawn up by the National Water Management Board. These maps concern mainly the south of Poland, the river Dunajec. According to the Act, the water law of the municipality was obliged to include flood areas in local land use plans within 30 months from the date of the transfer of the maps. Since the flood risk areas identified in the flood risk maps differ significantly from those identified in the study setting out the areas of direct flood risk developed by the RZGW director, both municipalities and private bodies submitted a number of comments to the National Water Management Board regarding the need to verify the accuracy of the boundaries of areas of particular flood risk. On 16 December 2015, the Sejm adopted an amendment to the Water Law Act. The most important change was the removal of the obligation to take account of flood risk maps when drawing up local land-use plans, spatial planning plans of voivodships, the concept of spatial development of the country and decisions on the conditions of construction and land-use. This obligation has been abolished, but according to the Law of 27 March 2003 on spatial planning and development, both spatial planning and local land-use plans must identify areas of particular risk of flooding. Therefore, despite the amendment to the Article. 88 Water rights areas of particular flood risk, including those identified in the current flood risk maps, must be included in the planning documents. There is therefore an inconsistency between the provisions of the Water Law and the Land Planning and Development Act. Regardless of the above, it draws attention to the fact that the coverage of flood flooding for the Dunajec catchment area as defined on flood risk maps developed by the President of the National Water Management Board is much larger than the range determined in the analysis of the RZGW in Krakow. The flood risk maps have been developed, as is apparent from the letters of the KZGW, based on the flows from the 1951-1993 period without taking into account the work of the Czorsztyn-Niedzica water reservoirs. Nevertheless, flood risk maps drawn up by the President of the KZGW and not detailed maps of the RZGW are mandatory when issuing release decisions. This is due to the fact that many of the investment activities in the Danube basin require decisions to be taken exempt from the water bans where accurate analyses do not qualify these areas for flood-related areas. The process of obtaining slow-down decisions is long and in this case is the same as for investments actually located in flood areas. In addition, the issuing of exemption decisions shall have effect on the basis of Article. 122 Water laws need to apply for another decision, this time for water law permission to build facilities and carry out works in flood areas, which are in fact not at risk of flooding. In this way, the construction process related to many investments in the region extends significantly at the approval stage of the documentation and its costs are increased. It is therefore necessary, as soon as possible, to adapt the provisions of the Spatial Planning and Development Act so that they are consistent with the amended provisions of the Article. 88 Water laws. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u404	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On February 23rd, we celebrated the National Day for Combating Depression. According to World Health Organization data, depression is the fourth most serious disease in the world and one of the main causes of suicide. The purpose of the event is to disseminate knowledge about depression and other related disorders, to raise awareness of this disease and its symptoms, to diagnose and treat it. Depression is a long-term disorder of mood and emotion that manifests itself not only with sadness and depression, but also with lack of concentration, loss of interest, suicidal thoughts, low self-esteem, sleep disorders. The Holy Father John Paul II said that depression often leads to an existential and spiritual crisis, which expresses the loss of meaning in life and exposes the human, psychological and spiritual vulnerability that society causes in part. Clinical depression is a disorder that should not be underestimated. Specialized therapy is usually indicated. But often a good mood depends on ourselves. We have the opportunity to make ourselves feel better. Many people may simply complain of a worse mood that does not meet the criteria for identifying depression. Knowing what can be strengthened or supplemented in our lives significantly protects against depression. During preventive counseling, you can learn how to protect yourself from this disease in everyday life. It is worth remembering that every disease and disorder requires a proper diagnosis, and the correct diagnosis can only be made by the person who is well prepared for it. In case of mental problems, this is a psychiatric doctor. In addition to psychiatric assistance, psychological and psychotherapeutic assistance is worthwhile. Medical care, as well as psychological assistance, can help a person in depression faster and more efficiently return to mental balance. Participation in therapy and working on their mental problems are desirable for people suffering from recurrent disorders, because they allow them to consolidate positive thinking patterns and give the sufferer the opportunity to learn about their inner experiences and feelings. It is very important to disseminate health-promoting attitudes in the field of mental health and to increase public awareness of the possibility of early identification and prevention of depressive disorders, as well as to increase the availability of preventive methods by disseminating information on available medical benefits such as psychoeducation, psychological assistance, psychotherapy, psychiatric consultations, dietary and lifestyle guidance. In order to increase public awareness of depression and thus to emphasise the need to take preventive and intervention measures in this area, I am making this statement. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2016-02-25-sejm-12-2.u408	Declaration on the 185th anniversary of the Battle of Olszynka Grochowska. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! February 25th this year We celebrate the 185th anniversary of the battle for Olszyna Grochowska. It was one of the most bloody battles of the November Uprising. The battle at Olszynka Grochowska was fought in the pre-fields of Warsaw's Prague. The concentration of Polish troops in the Olszynka grocery store under the village of Grochów, located near the capital, was to dissuade hostile troops from attacking Warsaw and stopping the momentum of attack. The forces of the insurgent troops were exhausted after many fights, such as. Under the Stokes, the Good and the Wawr. The march to Warsaw of the field marshal Ivan Dybicza was to thwart the Russians much less numerous Polish army. The first occasional attacks on insurgent troops took place on 20 February. However, they were effectively resisted, which gave Poles hope of victory and faith in their strength. The decisive attack of the Russians took place only a few days after the order of the frontal attack on Polish forces grouped in the forest of Olszynka was given. The operation began with an artillery fire on Polish positions. The Russians used the infantry for the proper attack. The first attack of the enemy was resisted. However, the vast majority of the Tsar army was exploited by their commander. Yet another attempt to break the Polish resistance line also failed. The fierce and uneven fight lasted for the next few hours. Further assaults of the overwhelming forces of the infantry and the charge of the larger cavalry did not lead to a break in the spirit of the fighting of Poles, although the forces of the insurgents were systematically exhausted. The situation changed from minute to minute, dramatically increasing the balance of the dead in the skirmish. The critical moment of the battle was the decision of Dybich to send his squadrons at the same time attacking the right and left wing of the insurgent troops. The Poles, forming quadrilaterals, resisted the dangerous charges of the Russians. The extraordinary persistence of the Polish defense and high losses of the enemy caused the Tsarist troops to turn around and abandon the attack of the fortifications of Prague, for which the insurgents withdrew. According to historians, the outcome of the battle for Olszynka Grochowska remains unresolved, but here we can talk about the triumph of Poles. The Poles' defeat in this fight could lead to the slaughter of Warsaw's inhabitants similar to that of the Kościuszko insurrection period. Heroism and fierce resistance of Poles did not allow the enemy to enter the capital. About 7 thousand people died on the Polish side during the battle. soldiers, among the enemy, the number of killed has reached about 12 thousand. persons. General Franciszek Żymirski was killed in the fight, and the actual commander-in-chief - General Józef Chłopicki - was seriously injured. The soldiers of the 4th century proved to be a special fortitude. The Line Infantry regiment. This battle was an inspiration for Stanisław Wyspiański to write a drama entitled "Warsawianka." In 1916 a wooden cross stood in the place of the battle - a monument to the battle of Olszynka Grochowska, 15 years later the cross was replaced with metal. Today, reconstructions of the fighting of insurgents are organized to commemorate the next anniversaries of the battle. Thank you.
